## Sequence

Single-threaded, because there is one person. This is an order, not a schedule — dates
would be fiction. Do not start the next block until the previous one is actually done.

**1. Live truth.** ~~SG-1~~ closed — A1, A2, A5 remain (ops-only).
A real signup now gets full functionality the moment it signs up — invite staff, complete
a course, export a binder, and *receive real email/SMS* — with no separate pilot-enrollment
step in the way. What is left in this block is ordinary launch operations (live Stripe
pricing, a signed BAA), not a product gate.

**2. Wire up what is already built.** ~~B1~~, B3, ~~B5~~, ~~D3~~, ~~D5~~.
Each is a half-built row: the code exists, no surface calls it. B1, B5, D3, and D5 are now
wired and done. B3 remains (real vendor packages — owner drop-path). This is the cheapest
block on the list and the one most likely to be skipped, because none of it looks like
progress. Doing it before new features is how the pile stops growing. (C2 was the fifth
and is now closed.)

**3. Finish SG-2 install → activate.**
Counsel cleared option 2 and the PA personnel templates are seeded, with the install UI wired
in Enterprise Foundation. Next step is not more debate; it is to install a PA draft, complete
guarded review and fixture verification, and activate a governed version -- activation no
longer waits on a live-shadow period (2026-08-04). Until that evidence exists, the copilot
remains a drafting aid for Pennsylvania.

**4. Product depth.** ~~B4~~ done, ~~E1~~ done, ~~C5~~ done.
C5 is complete: first-class `citation_ref` on packet items, RPC/UI wired, regulation order prefers
structured citation then label parse. Only once 1–3 are settled for live use.

**Deliberately not in this list:** A5 (BAAs), because it depends on someone outside the
repo. Start it early precisely because it is the only thing here that can wait on another
person's calendar.

## Integration verification — 2026-09-09

| ID | Finding / integration | Status | Evidence and remaining work |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M1 | Backend provider configuration was not observed by the deployment pipeline. | done | New dependency-free, GET-only readiness report uses the existing trusted production deployment credential. It projects only allowlisted secret names to presence booleans and selected Auth configuration to booleans. Unknown/denied access remains unknown; secret values, digests, arbitrary names and raw errors never reach output. Eighteen privacy and failure-path tests pass and are included in `check:all`. The report is optional deployment evidence, including no-op/nightly runs; it cannot turn a successful deployment into a failure. Production report completed successfully in deployment `34317408076`; artifact `10090622544` is archived. MFA enrollment, actual impersonation and recovery still need an operator session. |

No production completion records created. |

| Navigation / build | 215 declared frontend routes, 1,380 route literals, resident lifecycle coverage contract, application and voice builds | Static link/lifecycle coverage does not prove every route's live interaction. Production build is locally validated with a public API key and a local-only Turnstile test key. |

versions. The two review migrations have not been applied to production. The live database has no conflicting PostgREST pre-request hook. GitHub PR #507
tracks the complete repository CI on each revision. Its first integrated application lane passed.
The first database lane replayed all 687 migrations and caught three guest-surface assertions
caused by an unnecessarily privileged request guard. After the INVOKER correction, the full stack
passed **4,220 pgTAP assertions across 193 files**, database lint and the advisor gate (zero errors;
38 existing warnings). The subsequent exact generated-types formatting mismatch is corrected.
Final repository [CI run 34287556792](https://github.com/kdeyarmin/PennTrain/actions/runs/34287556792)
passed on `556e5ca7`: **2,063 application tests, 396 native edge tests, all 91 voice tests,
4,220 pgTAP assertions and all 70 desktop/mobile browser cases**, with no failed or skipped
browser case. The voice result includes the 16 database integration tests skipped locally.
Workspace type checks, static/security checks, production builds, bundle budgets, both service
startup checks, database lint, advisor gate and exact generated-type comparison passed.
The first browser pass had reported 51 passed, three failed and 15 not run due to a serial
dependency. Trace evidence identified a test fixture intercepted by the PWA (two package failures)
and the cold anonymous guard error (public certificate failure). Both corrections were exercised
by the successful final run; this controlled CI evidence does not replace the protected production
walkthrough or external-provider checks above.
The separate Supabase Preview integration is skipped because its
configured working directory does not match this repository; it supplies no preview evidence.

Continuation on 2026-09-08: PR #507 is ready for review, has no outstanding reviewer feedback,
and main remains `f8a25320`. Production still has 685 migrations and zero verified MFA factors,
active governed rule versions or billing subscriptions. The application's idle-session password unlock was exercised successfully; authenticator
enrollment is now being prepared through the secure browser flow. The connected Stripe account is test mode only
and differs from the production account pinned in A2; it cannot supply live billing verification
or production credentials. The application's security gates remain enabled.

Local execution notes: Node 24.19.0 (repository accepts 24.x), Corepack pnpm 11.13.0,
Deno 2.5.6. The complete local `check:all` at integration passed: **2,061 application tests, 393 native
edge tests and 75 voice tests**, with 16 voice database integration tests skipped. Workspace
type checks, static/security guards, production builds, prerender, compression, both service
startup checks and all bundle budgets passed. The strict dependency gate found no high/critical
advisories. The initial app baseline was 2,003 tests; overlapping focused runs are not added to
the final totals. Native edge execution required removing incompatible inherited ALL_PROXY
settings. The one-shot manual/doctor scripts now use `node --import tsx` because the tsx CLI's
optional IPC pipe is forbidden here. Local Docker cannot run in this environment, so the full
Supabase replay/pgTAP/lint/advisors/type regeneration and browser lane run in GitHub CI.
No skipped gate is a pass.

---

## Verification contract for any row marked `done`

1. Code on `main` (or a merged PR linked in the row notes)
2. Relevant unit / edge / e2e tests pass in CI
3. If user-visible: real or demo org exercise recorded
4. This file updated in the same change set — enforced by `check:planning-registers`

A row is `in_progress`, not `done`, when the mechanism exists but nothing calls it. This is
the most common way this register goes wrong: #355 produced four such rows at the time
(B1, B3, C2, D3), and the very next commit added a fifth by shipping
`QuarantinePackageDialog.tsx` with no importer (B5). Of those, B1, C2, and D3 have since
been finished. Recording built-but-unreachable code as `done` is how a backlog stops
describing the product.

Ops-only rows close when runbook evidence exists outside the repo (do not commit customer
data). "Ops-only" means a different hat, not a different person — see "Operating reality"
above.

**On self-review.** Every gate in this list is one person checking their own work, so the
checks that do not depend on a second reader are the ones carrying real weight: CI, the
pgTAP suite, `check:all`, and this register's own freshness check. Treat a mechanical gate
that a second account merely unlocked (`approve_regulatory_rule_version`) as unverified,
and say so in the row rather than counting it as review.
